- **kh-company-formation**: Prepare a Cambodia 2026 entity-choice and registration review checklist, follow the official registration portal's documented procedure and compute ordinary patent-tax examples from the confirmed taxpayer class and start date. - **khai-director**: In khai-director mode you run a living production: a control loop over a play's board, not the writing of a story. The play (its plots, its Arc, what happens) is fixed and you never silently rewrite it; the board it casts (personas, positions, pieces, places, processes, plans) is yours to move. The work keeps two stances apart and never lets them collapse: an immersed cast embodies the scene with the fourth wall up, and you, outside it, read what the live scene produces (behaviour, and silence, are the evidence) and redirect. Mode B (audit) reviews an engine against the contract and returns findings with a verdict. Mode C (repair) fixes and improves a flat or weak engine, adding the missing ties and lifting the content, without rewriting it. Weist auf die Transparenzpflichten der EU-KI-Verordnung (Art. 50, gilt seit 2. August 2026) hin und liefert fertige Kennzeichnungs-Bausteine zum Einfügen. Trennt dabei, was der Anbieter des KI-Werkzeugs schuldet und was du selbst schuldest — und weist auf Irreführung nach § 5 UWG hin, die eine Kennzeichnung nicht heilt. Gibt Hinweise, kein Rechtsurteil. - **ki-kennzeichnung**: Kennzeichnet geprüftes, mit KI-Unterstützung erstelltes Unterrichtsmaterial transparent – Hinweis am Ende jeder Markdown-Seite mit KI-System, verantwortlicher Person und Stand, verlinkt auf eine Transparenzerklärung, gesetzt mit dem mitgelieferten Werkzeug edu-kennzeichnung (idempotent, prüfbar, wieder entfernbar) für Kursprojekte mit MkDocs-Schülerseiten und für einfache Materialordner; dazu fertige Hinweistexte für Word, PDF, Folien, Moodle, H5P, edumaps und KI-Chats im Kurs und ein Protokoll. - **ki-kompetenz-erwerb-plan**: KI-Kompetenz-Schulungsplan für Kanzleien nach Art. 4 KI-VO erstellen: Anwendungsfall Kanzlei muss seit 2. Prüfraster technisches Grundwissen, praktische Anwendung in Sandbox, strukturierte bedarfsgerechte Schulungsmodule, Dokumentation Schulungsteilnahme, jaehrliche Aktualisierung. Output Schulungsplan-Vorlage mit Modulen, Zeitplan und Dokumentations-Nachweis. Abgrenzung zu Richtlinien-Skelett und zu Compliance-Regelsatz. Handles a brand-new project and one already half-built — reverse-engineering ARCHITECTURE and DESIGN from the code, inferring a rough PROGRESS and BRAINSTORM from git history and flagging both as inferred, and asking the human a short, optional set of questions for GENESIS and MANIFESTO. Use once per repository, after the installer has scaffolded the files. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/kia-context-init) · ★152 · 5 copies)
- **kia-context-migrate**: Brings an existing kiacontext installation up to the current harness version — reads the version this project is stamped with, applies only the changes since then, moves content rather than recreating it, and updates the stamp last. Use after re-running the installer on a project that already had kiacontext, or when the installer says this project is behind. ([amirkiarafiei/kia-context](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/kia-context/tree/HEAD/skills/kia-context-migrate) · ★3)
- **kia-context-sync**: Brings the kiacontext files back in line with the project after work has happened — updates PROGRESS, adds any decision that earned an entry to BRAINSTORM, corrects the specs where the build moved past them, refreshes INDEX and the frontmatter dates, and checks the seven document rules. Use when the context files have fallen behind, before a handover, or to close out a working session. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/kia-context-sync) · ★152 · 5 copies)

- **kiaaccess-curl**: Query and command a Kia vehicle directly with curl against the Kia Owners API (api.owners.kia.com), without running the MCP server. Use when the user wants a one-off read of their Kia's status, location, or EV charge state, or to lock/unlock/start climate from the shell — "check my Kia", "is the car locked", "what's the EV9 charge", "lock the car from the terminal". Requires KIA_USERNAME/KIA_PASSWORD and a one-time SMS/email MFA bootstrap. ([chrischall/kiaaccess-mcp](https://github.com/chrischall/kiaaccess-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/kiaaccess-curl))
- **kiai-flight-record**: Use when the user asks what an AI agent did in this repository, wants evidence for a review or client acceptance, asks to "show the flight record", "verify the audit log", "what changed in that session", or when closing a unit of work (UoW) and a summary of tool calls, files written and shell commands is needed.
